#5222. Ripple Takes the Upper Hand Against Ethereum's Ethena in Stablecoin Race

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

Ripple USD (RLUSD) now tops the chart for short-term volume growth on decentralized exchanges (DEXs), outpacing Ethena's USDe. RWA Foundation, citing Token Terminal data, shows RLUSD's DEX trading volume jumped by $127.4 million over the past seven days.

USDe, the synthetic dollar issued by Ethena, has a bigger market cap of $4.97 billion at press time. Its DEX volume grew by $71.4 million, giving RLUSD a $56 million lead for the week.

Other major DeFi stablecoins, Usual's USD0 and Aave's GHO among them, stayed below $20 million.

Ethena offers roughly 5% yield through sUSDe staking. According to Ethena's documentation, the main risk of its delta-neutral model, which relies on short positions in perpetual futures, is prolonged negative funding rates.

USDe supply peaked above $14 billion in 2025. Ethena's fee switch and ENA buybacks are set to start only once the 14-day average USDe supply reaches $7.5 billion.

RLUSD is issued under the supervision of the New York State Department of Financial Services (NYDFS) and backed 100% by cash and short-term U.S. Treasuries, with monthly attestations of its reserves.

BNY acts as the custodian of RLUSD reserves.

In late October, the US Office of the Comptroller of the Currency (OCC) gave final approval to Ripple National Trust Bank. The bank will manage RLUSD reserves under federal oversight.

Besides it, RLUSD is available in Spark's Stablecoin FX pool on Uniswap v4, which lets corporate players make multi-million-dollar swaps.

According to RWA Foundation, about 92% of RLUSD turnover on the XRP Ledger (XRPL) runs through direct pairs with XRP.

Ripple also expanded its partnership with Robinhood. According to the companies, Bitstamp will use RLUSD on the XRP Ledger for settlement, while Robinhood plans to offer RLUSD to its retail customers by the end of the year.

At the same time, Coinbase added RLUSD to Coinbase Prime, Coinbase Exchange and its retail app, with 1:1 USD conversion on the XRP Ledger, Ethereum and Base.

At press time, RLUSD's market cap stands at $2.69 billion, roughly half USDe's size. The stablecoin started 2026 at about $1.28 billion.

#5221. Bitcoin CPI Reaction: 10 of 13 Cool Inflation Reports Triggered an Hourly Gain – Can Inflation Data Reverse the Downtrend?

Bitcoin CPI reaction: October CPI lands at 8:30 a.m. ET, core seen at 0.22%. Cool prints lifted BTC in the first hour 10 times out of 13.

The U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics publishes October’s consumer price index (CPI) on Tuesday, November 10, at 8:30 a.m. in New York, or 13:30 UTC. The Bitcoin CPI reaction that follows could set the tone for the week. The CPI is the main U.S. measure of inflation, and it will shape the Federal Reserve’s next rate decision.

Economists expect core prices, which leave out food and energy, to have risen about 0.22% in the month. That would be a slowdown from September’s 0.33%.

Economists expect growth in the core CPI to have moderated in October to around 0.22%, though the full range of estimates is broad enough to round down to 0.1% or round up to 0.3%.

Due to the government shutdown in Oct 2025, there's no tidy year-earlier figure. pic.twitter.com/CFM7ikw8fO

— Nick Timiraos (@NickTimiraos) November 9, 2026

Bitcoin trades near $74,400, still close to Sunday’s two-month low of $73,510.

CPI days move crypto. We checked Bitcoin’s first hour after the last 24 releases. When core inflation came in at 0.2% or lower, BTC rose in that hour 10 times out of 13. When it came in at 0.3% or higher, it rose only 4 times out of 10.

So which way will today’s number push Bitcoin?

The forecasts point to a calmer month. Seventeen banks and research firms surveyed by The Wall Street Journal see a median rise of 0.25% for headline CPI and 0.22% for core. Journal reporter Nick Timiraos posted the table on X.

That spread matters. The government reports CPI rounded to one decimal place. With forecasts this spread out, the core reading could print anywhere from 0.1% to 0.3%. A 0.3% would match September’s pace and read as inflation that is not cooling.

The yearly comparison will also be messy. The government shutdown in October 2025 meant no CPI was published for that month, so there is no clean year-over-year figure.

Market Intelligence: Crypto Analyst Predicts Best Low-Cap Crypto

This is the last CPI report the Federal Reserve will see before its December 8-9 meeting. Traders already expect a rate hike: on Tuesday morning, Polymarket gives an 86.5% chance of a 0.25-point increase and 11.5% for no change.

Higher rates tend to weigh on Bitcoin. They make cash and bonds pay more, which pulls money away from assets that pay no interest, like crypto.

A cool reading could shave those odds and give Bitcoin room to bounce. A hot one would lock in the hike.

DISCOVER: Fed Meeting Today: Traders Price a 95% Hold and a 79% December Hike. What Does It Mean for Crypto?

We measured the Bitcoin CPI reaction on Binance. We took the 24 CPI reports published since October 2024 and measured Bitcoin’s move in the hour after each 8:30 a.m. release.

The pattern is a lean, not a rule. The biggest drop came on February 12, 2025, when core rose 0.4% and BTC lost 1.9% in an hour. Last month’s 0.3% reading cost it 1.4%.

So far it has. BTC trades at $74,398 on Binance, about 1.2% above Sunday’s low.

On the daily chart, Bitcoin sits below both of its exponential moving averages (EMAs), averages that weight recent prices more. The 200-day EMA is at $76,481 and the 50-day EMA at $79,128.

For anyone tracking the Bitcoin CPI reaction, the first hour after 8:30 a.m. is the one to watch. In 20 of the last 24 reports, Bitcoin ended the next 24 hours moving in the same direction as in that first hour.

#5220. XRP Ledger Unveils Fee-Free Quantum-Proof Upgrade as Ripple Races Toward 2028 Security Deadline: Here’s What’s Coming

The XRP Ledger is preparing for a major security overhaul as quantum computing moves from a theoretical concern to a growing priority for the crypto industry. With Ripple targeting 2028 for its post-quantum security transition, developers are now unveiling a proposed quantum-resistant upgrade that promises to protect users against future attacks without introducing additional transaction fees.

The development could mark a significant turning point for the network, raising questions about what exactly is changing, how the upgrade will work, and whether XRP holders will need to do anything before the looming security deadline.

The XRP community has a new development to watch as the XRP Ledger Foundation outlines plans to strengthen the network against future quantum computing risks. The proposed upgrade would introduce quantum-resistant accounts, giving users an additional layer of protection against emerging cryptographic threats while keeping transaction costs unchanged.

Although the feature has yet to be implemented, the proposed upgrade would allow XRP Ledger users to register NIST-approved ML-DSA-44 quantum-resistant signing keys and deactivate their existing authentication keys. This would give accounts an additional layer of protection against potential quantum computing attacks, particularly those whose public keys have already been exposed on the network.

Importantly, users could adopt the enhanced security system without changing how they store, hold, or access their XRP. The concern stems from how transactions are authorized on the network. When users sign transactions through their cryptocurrency wallets, their public keys can become exposed, potentially creating an attack vector if sufficiently advanced quantum computers emerge.

Such technology could eventually undermine existing cryptographic protections, putting accounts with exposed public keys at risk. The XRP Ledger Foundation’s proposed solution is designed to address this vulnerability before quantum computing advances to the point where such attacks become practically achievable.

Beyond the additional protection, the proposal has another notable selling point: affordability. Users would not have to pay higher transaction fees to benefit from the enhanced security. In other words, XRP holders could strengthen their accounts against emerging quantum threats without incurring additional transaction costs, making the proposed upgrade particularly appealing to the community.

Although the upgrade has not yet gone live, the team has outlined its next steps, with a migration study planned for the first half of 2027. The research will explore how existing XRPL accounts can move to the new quantum-resistant security framework, including the transition process and what account holders would need to do to adopt the upgraded protection.

Developers are also addressing discrepancies in how the proposed system generates cryptographic keys across its Python, JavaScript, and server implementations, a necessary step toward ensuring consistent and reliable performance across the network.

As ZyCrypto previously reported, Ripple has outlined a comprehensive four-phase roadmap to make the XRP Ledger, its decentralized Layer-1 blockchain, resistant to potential quantum computing attacks, with full quantum readiness targeted for 2028.

#5219. Schiff: Saylor's STRC Is 'Dead'

Earlier today, gold bug Peter Schiff claimed that Strategy executive chairman Michael Saylor was trying to prop up the floundering investment product by carrying out heavy stock buybacks.

"Michael, STRC is dead. You have been keeping it alive on artificial life support with your buybacks," he wrote.

According to Schiff, Strategy is running out of money to prop up its preferred stock. The ongoing Bitcoin market crash is going to aggravate the situation even further, he argued.

"But you're running out of cash. And the selling pressure on STRC will increase as Bitcoin crashes," Schiff wrote.

The Bitcoin bear went on to criticize the generous 12% annualized dividend yield of STRC, saying that ultimately the yield wouldn't be enough to cover the risks of the product.

"It won't be nearly enough yield to cover the risk. 12%," he warned.

The stern comments from Schiff followed a typical bullish tweet from Saylor. In his post, Saylor claimed that AI could not mint new Bitcoin.

"AI can make me rap. It can't print Bitcoin," the cofounder of Strategy wrote.

Strategy's Variable Rate Series A Perpetual Stretch Preferred Stock (STRC) seeks to provide regular dividend income to investors while trading near its $100 face value.

Currently, STRC offers a 12% annual dividend yield, which Strategy has been working to keep steady in order to keep the stock trading near its face value.

However, maintaining the price stability of STRC has come at a big cost.

In its Q3 financial results on Oct. 29, Strategy reported that the firm had spent a total of $2.04 billion on repurchases of STRC as of Oct. 25.

Describing the heavy buybacks as a success, the firm said it had successfully put STRC on the road to financial recovery.

However, these buybacks have come under fire from Schiff, who questions whether the firm can keep propping up investor demand by repurchasing its shares forever.

In spite of the bearish comments from Schiff, Strategy isn't budging on its strategy of ramping up its Bitcoin holdings.

The firm's recent regulatory filing shows that Strategy bought another 1,036 BTC between Nov. 2 and Nov. 8.

The total cost of these purchases is estimated to be $81.9 million, with an average purchase price of $79,012 per BTC.

With the latest purchase, Strategy now holds an eye-watering 850,364 BTC.

#5218. Bitcoin, Gold & Productive Land: Your Shield Against Inflation: Anthony Pompliano

Popular crypto commentator Anthony Pompliano has shortlisted Bitcoin, Gold and Productive land to combat inflation in the uncertain times ahead. He has warned multiple times that the dollarized economy is in decline, and consumers need to protect their wealth by investing in scarce, useful assets that will hold their value well.

He used the term “money printer” in his tweet, which has often been used satirically to attack central banks for their money-printing spree. The short tweet sums up the ethos of the crypto-savvy investor class who have been clamoring for better wealth management for the better part of the last decade and a half. He, along with cypherpunks and other maximalists, believes the best defense against the onslaught of inflationary forces is to invest in assets that cannot be conjured out of thin air.

This is not the first time Pompliano has stated his preference for these three assets. Bitcoin is obviously the asset of choice for this particular demographic that emerged after the devastating 2008 financial crisis.

Gold is obviously another major asset that has withstood the test of time and has appreciated strongly in the last decade or so. Productive land is another asset with a strong future as the world faces uncertain times, with food insecurity at record levels.

The timing of the tweet is crucial, as investors look for orthodox and unorthodox ways to preserve or increase their wealth over time. The effects of large-scale stimulus and debt accumulation cannot be understated. Inflation is reducing the value of savings, and the interest-based economy can hardly keep up with it, let alone deliver a steady net increase.

Twitter responses to the post varied a lot. A large section of Pompliano’s followers said it was a good trio of assets and highlighted their explosive growth over the years. However, others criticized that it wasn’t easy to hold any of these three assets.

Gold requires custody, and holding it individually can be risky, too, with no yield. Bitcoin, on the other hand, requires a relatively tech-savvy mindset and can be volatile at times. Thirdly, land requires management, faces liquidity constraints, and can be affected by local economic or environmental factors. It is not a walk in the park to take advantage of any of these assets.

However, given the uncertain future, they are certainly worth considering, as we all know one thing: fiat is likely to get worse over time.

#5216. North Korean Hackers Flip Stolen Binance Funds Into NEAR Meme Coins

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

North Korea's TraderTraitor group has started laundering funds stolen from Binance through newly created meme tokens, according to Elliptic.

The $6.4 billion Binance hack took place in October 2026 and was attributed to TraderTraitor.

According to Elliptic, the hackers have used this technique on Tron and NEAR, while a similar approach has appeared on Solana.

Between Nov. 5 and 7, 2026, the hackers launched at least six of these tokens on NEAR: FUK, OLCCL, MEILI, MMLI, DABI and SINU. Launching one cost around 25 NEAR (about $90).

According to Elliptic, the hackers paired each token with NEAR in a liquidity pool on a decentralized exchange (DEX). Wallets holding stolen NEAR deposited it into the pool and received the new token. A second set of wallets then sold the token back into the pool and withdrew NEAR.

This way, the funds never move directly from the wallets holding stolen assets to the receiving wallets.

According to Elliptic, the receiving wallets look like ordinary traders who sold a token at a profit.

Each token got typical meme branding, from monkeys to an image of Elon Musk under an "Elon 2028 President" banner. Elliptic noted that Musk is not linked to the tokens.

Each token was sold for the first time less than three hours after launch, and at least 75% of its trading took place within one day. None of the six tokens has traded since Nov. 8, and the creators of four of them withdrew the remaining liquidity on that day.

In total, 117,000 NEAR (about $421,000) moved through the scheme. Most of it then went into stablecoins or to a cross-chain bridge.

Elliptic noted that five of the six token creators received funds from a wallet that had been selling the previous token 2 to 13 minutes before the new launch, and in this way the operation funded itself.

The only outside activity was a few small purchases of FUK on its first day, which accounted for less than 1% of its volume, Elliptic said.

According to Elliptic, the on-chain trail stays public, and the technique makes tracing the funds slower. The hackers can also create many tokens and abandon each one after use.

In 2025, after the Bybit hack, the same scheme moved at least $24 million through Tron. In that case, the hackers paired a worthless token with a stablecoin.

In the Binance case, Elliptic identified a similar token on Tron on Nov. 2. On Nov. 3, stolen SOL was swapped into existing thinly traded tokens on Solana and then sold for USDC.

Elliptic said that so far only a small part of the stolen funds has gone through meme tokens. About 11.8 million NEAR from the theft has not moved yet, and at press time, NEAR is trading at $3.60, which puts these funds at around $42.4 million.

Elliptic also advises compliance teams to check funds coming from liquidity pools, especially pools with newly created tokens.

"Laundering is a race to move funds before they are flagged," Elliptic added.

#5215. XRP Price Plunges to $1.05 After a Hot CPI Report: Will the Support Stand?

Ripple XRP fell to $1.05 after a hot CPI report. After 7 of the last 10 hot reports, XRP was higher a day later. Can $1.05 hold?

Ripple XRP fell to $1.05 on Tuesday within five minutes of the U.S. October consumer price index (CPI) report, which showed XRP USD price rising faster than economists expected.

XRP crypto traded at $1.0797 when the report came out at 8:30 a.m. in New York, or 13:30 UTC. By 13:35 UTC it had touched $1.0500 on Binance, a 2.8% drop and its lowest price since August 19. It has since bounced to about $1.07. Bitcoin fell with it, dropping to $73,525 in the same five minutes.

The drop puts XRP back on a level it has tested before. Between late June and mid-August, XRP traded around $1.05 for weeks. The level held the first time and broke the second.

So will the support stand this time, and what do the CPI numbers tell us?

BREAKING: October CPI inflation rises +0.4% month-over-month, above expectations of +0.3%.

Core CPI inflation rose +0.3%, also above expectations of +0.2%.

BLS will not be releasing year-over-year figures due to the October 2025 government shutdown.

— The Kobeissi Letter (@KobeissiLetter) November 10, 2026

Prices rose faster than forecast in October. The Bureau of Labor Statistics said consumer prices rose 0.4% in the month, against the 0.3% economists expected.

Core prices, which leave out food and energy because they swing the most, rose 0.3%. The forecast was 0.2%. Core inflation has now run at about 0.3% for three months in a row.

The report is missing one usual figure. The bureau did not publish a yearly inflation rate because it never collected prices in October 2025, during the government shutdown. That leaves the monthly numbers to carry the whole report.

Those monthly numbers matter for the Federal Reserve. Fed Governor Christopher Waller said in a speech this morning that he already leans toward a 0.25 percentage point rate hike in December. If core CPI came in around 0.3% again, he said, a 0.5 point hike “should be considered”.

Core CPI did come in at 0.3%. Before the release, traders saw almost no chance of a hike that large. Higher rates make cash and bonds pay more, which tends to pull money out of risky assets like XRP.

Market Intelligence: Crypto Analyst Predicts Next Crypto to Hit 1 Dollar

The first move after a hot report has often faded. We measured XRP’s price on Binance in the 24 hours after each of the last 24 CPI reports, from October 2024 to October 2026.

Ten of those reports had core inflation of 0.3% or higher, like today’s. After seven of them, XRP was higher 24 hours later. The median move was a 1.4% gain.

The record is weaker this year. Of the four hot reports in 2026, XRP was higher a day later only twice. After the last one, on October 14, it fell 4.4% in 24 hours.

The difference this time is the Fed. A hot report that only confirms a small hike is easier for traders to shrug off. One that puts a larger hike on the table, as Waller laid out, gives sellers more to work with.

EXPLORE: XRP Price Forecast: Ripple at Risk of Losing Key Support, $1 Next?

The level has a history this year. XRP has spent two long stretches around $1.05 since June.

Today’s low of $1.0500 sits right on that zone. The first test of a level often holds. Each new test tends to leave fewer buyers there.

XRP trades at about $1.07 on Binance after the CPI drop, down 2.8% in 24 hours and 17% in a week. It is 71% below its July 2025 record of $3.65.

On the daily chart, where each candle covers one day, XRP sits well below both of its exponential moving averages (EMAs), averages that give recent prices more weight. The 50-day EMA stands at $1.294 and the 200-day EMA at $1.350.

For Ripple XRP, the first test of $1.05 has held so far. The daily close decides it: a close above $1.05 keeps the support alive, while a close below it opens the way to $1.

Market Intelligence: Crypto Analyst Predicts Top Solana Meme Coins to Bid

#5214. Shiba Inu (SHIB) Holder Count Surges Amid Severe Price Correction

The leading meme-based crypto asset, Shiba Inu, has continued to face a severe price drop amid prolonged crypto market volatility, yet its user activity has remained positive.

While uncertainty caused by its frequent price fluctuations has persisted, it appears that new users have remained enthusiastic about the leading meme token project, and they have continued to join the Shiba Inu ecosystem.

Latest on-chain data from Etherscan shows that the Shiba Inu holder count has now reached 1,698,454 as of today, November 9, following the consistent growth seen in the past day.

This means that a total of 2,025 new SHIB holders have been added to the ecosystem since the new month began, signaling sustained user growth even while its price performance has been extremely weak so far in November.

While the consistent increase in SHIB holders has been accompanied by a persistent decline in its price and slowed on-chain activity, it remains uncertain whether the rise in the number of holders could be pointed to increasing accumulation.

The surge in SHIB holders has drawn attention from market analysts as it comes after the network witnessed slow adoption late last month as nearly 2,500 wallet addresses holding the meme coin disappeared within just two days.

Nonetheless, it appears that interest in the meme token is picking pace again as a decent number of users continue to join in everyday, positioning it to hit the long-anticipated 1,700,000 holder milestone this month as Shiba Inu currently needs to add only 1,546 more holders to hit this key level.

#5213. Why Fidelity calls Solana ‘an emerging piece of financial market infrastructure’

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

From a technical standpoint, SOL has been the worst performer in the Q4 rally. It’s currently trading at just a few points above $50 and has recently fallen below $70 once again, resulting in another wave of weak hands taking profits. $50 is back on the radar.

In this light, the focus shifts to Fidelity’s latest report, in which the firm called Solana,

An emerging piece of financial market infrastructure.

According to the report, Solana’s trading volume was about 15% of the big five U.S. stock exchanges combined this year. Meanwhile, its trade count has also grown nearly 100x since 2022, compared with just 1.76x growth across major U.S. stock exchanges.

In essence, Solana’s competitive edge is becoming increasingly tied to on-chain trading activity.

From a structural perspective, this provides more weight to Solana’s DeFi growth. Increased trade will drive higher transaction fees and overall revenue within the Solana ecosystem. This provides a clearer utility story for SOL.

As a result, SOL’s long-term value may be more dictated by on-chain demand.

If this proves true, SOL may have strong fundamentals underlying the price weakness. An increase of network activity will help maintain the long-term outlook, therefore creating a potential longer-term investment.

So the bigger question now will be whether increased macro liquidity can cause the on-chain growth to filter through to Solana’s [SOL] price.

Liquidity and capital flows into risk assets tend to move together.

This is evident in the stablecoin market, where total market cap has slipped below $300 billion following a 1.7% weekly decline. This marks the first drop below this level since the market rallied toward $320 billion ahead of last October’s crash.

The result? Solana breaking below the $50 level.

There is still not enough liquidity in the stablecoin market to create strong bullish momentum, although there are some positive signs that liquidity is increasing.

Circle has just created $250 million more USDC on the Solana blockchain, and the U.S. M2 money supply has gone up $113 billion to record levels of $23.41 trillion.

In essence, macro liquidity is expanding, potentially setting the stage for capital to flow back into crypto.

This places Solana in an interesting position. Whilst price movement has been poor, on-chain activity and liquidity overall are showing signs of improvement.

Should capital flow back into the market, SOL’s increasing on-chain activity could be a major factor in driving the next rally.

In this context, Solana’s 15% share of trading volume could be just the start. Increased macro liquidity can increase on-chain activity, which will increase the fees on the network, thus improving revenue.

An increase in capital flowing into the crypto market will put Solana in a great position to turn this growing adoption into its next major growth phase.

Fidelity highlights Solana’s growing adoption, with trading volume reaching 15% of the five major U.S. stock exchanges combined.

Rising USDC supply and record U.S. liquidity could bring fresh capital into crypto, boosting Solana’s network activity and supporting SOL’s next rally.

#5212. Charles Schwab Expands Crypto Trading to SOL, AVAX and LINK, XRP Not Included

American brokerage giant Charles Schwab has announced that it is expanding its crypto trading platform, Schwab Crypto, with the addition of Solana (SOL), Avalanche (AVAX) and Chainlink (LINK) for trading.

The three new coins expand the existing list of two cryptocurrencies, Bitcoin (BTC) and Ethereum (ETH), that can be traded directly. This now means that users can trade five cryptocurrencies directly on the platform.

Notably, however, XRP, one of the largest cryptocurrencies by market cap, is still missing from the list of available coins.

In its official press release, Schwab's head of digital assets, Joe Vietri, underlined the brokerage's commitment to further expanding its crypto trading options.

Also, the company confirmed that it will be adding more cryptocurrencies and digital assets going forward. But it didn't specify whether this includes XRP.

Although XRP is not offered directly on the Schwab Crypto trading platform, it is still available to Schwab users in one way or another.

Charles Schwab offers standard and micro futures contracts on XRP through its thinkorswim platform.

In July 2025, the company pointed out the availability of XRP futures with the ticker /XRP and also smaller micro contracts with the ticker /MXP.

It further expanded its derivatives trading platform with the addition of almost 24/7 trading for certain crypto futures, including Bitcoin, Ethereum, Solana and XRP, in June 2026.

Charles Schwab's aggressive approach to the crypto industry has been noticeable throughout 2026.

First, the company officially announced Schwab Crypto on April 16. In this announcement, it outlined its plans to introduce direct trading of Bitcoin and Ethereum for retail users.

The next major development came on Oct. 15, when Rick Wurster, the CEO of Charles Schwab, confirmed the introduction of crypto transfers.

The platform now allows eligible customers to transfer eligible crypto assets to and from external wallets through the Schwab Crypto platform. However, this is not yet available to all customers. Access is being rolled out gradually.

#5211. “Tired After 7 Years”: Why This Veteran XRP Investor Considers Selling Everything at Current Prices

XRP’s potential run to $10 may not be enough to keep every longtime holder invested. In a striking admission, a long-time XRP investor has revealed plans to sell his entire position at current prices, despite the token’s much-anticipated upside target. The comments have sparked fresh debate over whether years of waiting will ultimately pay off for XRP holders or whether some are ready to walk away before the next parabolic rally.

In a recent post on X, Genfinity founder Ryan Solomon, known as King Solomon, expressed growing frustration with XRP after holding onto the cryptocurrency for seven years. Despite the community’s long-standing hopes of a major explosion toward $10, he suggested he may no longer be willing to wait for that milestone, revealing plans to sell his entire XRP position at current market prices and citing the significant opportunity cost of staying invested so long.

“When XRP eventually hits $10, many won’t sell,” Solomon said. “I’m likely selling everything at current prices. Tired after 7 years. Too much opportunity cost,” Solomon wrote.

He also expressed frustration with what he described as shifting narratives and gaslighting surrounding XRP. After years of waiting, he seems increasingly unconvinced that continuing to hold the payments-focused token is worth sacrificing other investment opportunities.

The comments carry added weight given Solomon’s years of involvement in researching XRP and the broader digital-asset infrastructure sector through Genfinity. His willingness to consider walking away after seven years suggests that even the prospect of XRP eventually reaching the much-coveted $10 level may no longer be enough to keep some longtime holders committed.

In simple words, his stance highlights the frustration that can build even among long-term participants in the XRP ecosystem. That sentiment comes as XRP hovers around $1.10, according to CoinGecko data, marking a decline of approximately 51% over the past year and highlighting a prolonged downturn that is testing investors’ patience.

Another longtime XRP community member going by the name Nikolas echoed Solomon’s frustration, admitting that holding out for the $5-$10 price range was one of his biggest investment mistakes. He also criticized the XRP community for what he described as a growing inability to think critically, suggesting that many holders have become too attached to bullish expectations.

The investor further questioned the relationship between Ripple Labs and XRP, arguing that the company’s business interests appear increasingly disconnected from the token’s performance. In his view, Ripple may be prioritizing its corporate growth and investors while XRP holders struggle to see comparable benefits from the company’s expansion.

Meanwhile, crypto commentator Good Evening Crypto suggested that Solomon’s concerns may reflect a much broader sentiment among longtime XRP holders. He said a friend working in private equity, who has held XRP since 2021, recently expressed similar frustrations about the opportunity cost of staying invested.

Although Good Evening Crypto said he does not share the same view, he acknowledged that Solomon’s decision to consider selling deserves attention given his experience and standing in the community. He suggested that thousands of other XRP holders may be quietly reaching the same conclusion but have yet to voice their frustrations publicly.

Together, these comments point to growing discontent among some longtime XRP holders, raising questions about whether the token’s anticipated upside still justifies the years of waiting.

#5210. Coachella 2027 Lineup Leaks: Polymarket Odds Reveal the Top 3 Favorites

Home » Crypto Market News » Coachella 2027 Lineup Leaks: Polymarket Odds Reveal the Top 3 Favorites

Industry leaks point to major pop and rock icons—here is who prediction markets expect to officially take the stage.

Rumors about the Coachella 2027 lineup picked up steam after reports and social media posts pointed to SZA, Dua Lipa, and Tame Impala as the festival’s possible headliners.

While the music festival’s promoter Goldenvoice has yet to confirm any performers, crypto-based prediction markets reacted instantly to the industry buzz.

On Polymarket, the leading Web3 prediction platform, capital flowed rapidly into event contracts as crypto traders rushed to price in the latest reporting.

Industry buzz began picking up momentum after reports from media outlets, including TMZ, indicated that talks were underway with Dua Lipa and Tame Impala.

The speculation spiked further when R&B star SZA seemingly corroborated the rumors online, pointing toward a headlining trio representing three distinct corners of modern pop, R&B, and psychedelic rock.

Reports also suggest that festival organizers had approached other massive acts—including BTS, Oasis, Radiohead, and Rage Against the Machine—before cementing focus on the current trio.

Crypto-based prediction markets have become a real-time barometer of public sentiment when entertainment leaks surface. The rumors have also played out on Polymarket, where odds shift instantly as new information emerges.

Polymarket’s latest odds show strong confidence in the leaked trio.

As of November 10, SZA has a 74% implied probability of appearing on the lineup. Dua Lipa follows at 65%, with $9,917 in trading volume—the highest individual total among all listed artists—marking a potential milestone for her first solo Coachella headlining performance. Tame Impala sits close behind at 61%.

Beyond the top three, other artists are also drawing bets. BLACKPINK has a 45% implied probability, followed by Drake at 28%.

Other names further down the list include Travis Scott at 16%, Harry Styles at 15%, Daft Punk at 13%, BTS at 7%, and Justin Bieber at 5%.

Trading volume across Coachella-related markets climbed within hours of the rumors spreading on social media. The market is scheduled to remain open until January 31, 2027.

The reaction to the Coachella 2027 lineup is a prime example of how prediction markets react to mainstream entertainment news long before official announcements drop. Traders aggregate information from media reports, social media leaks, and industry chatter to price in probabilities.

While these odds do not represent guaranteed confirmation from the promoter Goldenvoice, they offer a direct view into market consensus—one that shifts dynamically as fresh details emerge.

One of the world’s biggest music festivals is no stranger to blockchain. In 2022, Coachella auctioned ten lifetime-pass NFTs. The collection raised roughly $1.4 million. After FTX’s collapse that November, many holders lost access to their keys, as assets kept on the exchange became stuck.

In 2024, the festival tried again with OpenSea on Avalanche, reissuing NFTs to affected buyers, though demand for the first new collection was initially weak.

Speaking to his followers on X, the well-known economic and capital markets analyst warned of a potential Bitcoin plunge.

His remarks suggested that BTC is set to lose resilience due to recent events that have eroded investor confidence and pushed crypto prices lower.

Based on his market observation, the analyst noted that since the recently concluded 2026 midterm elections, U.S. stock markets have surged around 12% while Bitcoin dropped by 4.6%. Over the same period, gold and silver prices have risen by 5% and 7%, respectively.

According to Peter Schiff, price hikes in equities, gold, and silver indicate that Wall Street and traditional investors are celebrating the election outcome, marked by Democrats winning control of both the House and Senate.

Initially, investors feared a lengthy political battle after the Democratic sweep. In short, investors expect a divided Congress to clamp down on government spending and borrowing.

Also, the yield on the benchmark 10-year Treasury note dropped by 4.91% following the Democratic victory. This means lower Treasury yields support stock valuations by reducing borrowing costs and eroding bond returns, making them less attractive relative to equities and precious metals. This explains strong gains in U.S. stocks and rare metals.

Bitcoin weakened after the midterm election, noting that the timing indicates that the vote and the subsequent Binance hack impacted investor sentiment. Today, BTC trades at $73,135, after losing a crucial support at $75,000 and falling from its recent $80,000 peak.

Schiff is aware that Bitcoin always falls after midterm elections. He knows more drops are coming now that the asset has lost its key support at $75k. #5206. Ripple's Schwartz Debunks XRP Price Myth

David Schwartz, the former chief technology officer at Ripple and one of the creators of the XRP Ledger (XRPL), has dispelled one of the most common myths regarding the key factors that could impact the price of XRP.

The former Ripple CTO took to the X social media platform to challenge the idea that the use of XRP as the native gas token of the XRP Ledger could be a major factor in determining the price of the cryptocurrency.

"I don't think anybody who knows anything has said that the use of XRP as gas will be a major driver of the price of XRP," Schwartz wrote.

The comment was made as the Ripple veteran took part in the ever-growing debate centered around the poor price performance of XRP and the place of the cryptocurrency in Ripple's ever-expanding ecosystem.

The former Ripple CTO directed the audience's attention to a post from November 2025, in which he explained his thoughts on how mass adoption of the XRP Ledger could lead to greater value for the ledger's native cryptocurrency.

"The increased use and adoption of XRPL will add value to XRP by its privileged place on the ledger," Schwartz said.

He emphasized that XRP has several unique properties, including the lack of counterparty risk, the fact that it's jurisdictionless, and its privileged place in the network's autobridging features.

In addition, Schwartz argued that it would be hard to imagine the XRP Ledger succeeding on a mass scale without XRP capturing a significant portion of the value created by such success.

"I really do think it's hard to think we could end up in a world where XRPL is successful and XRP doesn't get a good fraction of that value," he said.

Nevertheless, Schwartz also said earlier that the XRP Ledger was not created with the sole intent of pushing the price of XRP higher. Instead, the original goal was to create a fast and cheap payment system with a native digital asset and a decentralized exchange feature.

Schwartz's recent comments come as frustration among XRP holders over the poor performance of the cryptocurrency is on the rise.

As of Monday, the Ripple-related token was trading at around the $1.09 level, down more than 12% in the last seven days.

Recently, Schwartz admitted that his influence on the market price of XRP is very small.

"Do I have anything to do with the price of XRP? Honestly? I guess probably not anything. But very little," he wrote during another interaction with critics.

But even if XRP is oversold, with RSI below 30, bulls might still stay sidelined. The XRP Ledger activated the fixCleanup3_4_0 amendment on November 4. It removed an exploit that, under certain conditions, let accounts authorized to burn an issuer's tokens also mint them. The native supply of XRP has not been affected, luckily, but the amendment is not a factor that can help XRP reach key market levels.

In a case, where bulls somehow wake up, a recovery towards $1.15 and $1.20 is the main priority, while a proper trend reversal would require a move toward the moving averages around $1.30–$1.35.

Whether buyers can defend the $1 level or not is the main test for the asset right now. Without substantial volume, a short-lived price bounce here won't change the situation as a whole, unfortunately.

NEAR entered November under pressure after losing much of its September advance. After briefly reaching approximately $6 in October, the token fell toward $3.66 on November 9, according to the daily NEAR/USDT chart.

That represented a decline of roughly 39% from the local peak and put NEAR back at an important moving-average support zone.

Trading activity has also weakened alongside the price. Volume remains substantially below the levels seen during September’s breakout, suggesting that buyers have not yet returned with comparable strength.

However, lower volume does not necessarily confirm that selling pressure has disappeared. NEAR continues to form lower highs, while recovery attempts remain below its shorter-term moving averages.

Institutional exposure remains part of the investment case. Bitwise’s spot NEAR ETF, launched on September 29, held approximately 15.69 million NEAR worth $58.21 million as of Nov. 5. The fund reported that 99% of its holdings were staked.

Hyperliquid keeps going down and has recently violated an important moving average. It's now trading at $75, going further into downtrend, despite the strong fundamental background around Hyperliquid: Hyperliquid Strategies disclosure and a potential approval by the U.S. futures commission.

The broader market access, however, isn't something that the majority of retail investors consider a bullish catalyst, unfortunately. The asset has not yet found a strong bullish case. It's unlikely to be institutional too.

Technically, the bounce of HYPE/USDT could happen on 200 EMA, especially with a volume uptick and the recovery of the market in general. However, the selling pressure still overweights the buying support, according to exchange flows.

The netflow stays negative, and Hyperliquid is only yet to catch up with the bullish flow of the market. RSI confirms the thesis, moving around 40 and suggesting a bearish momentum, or a sideways movement at least. Volume should also surge substantially.

Key price levels for HYPE are: $72–$73 as the closest support level, $68 as the longer-term moving average, and $78–$80 as the first resistance level. Several moving averages sit at approximately $81–$85, so a breakthrough there should become a foundation for a price recovery.

Stellar already lost an important psychological level of $0.15, and there's a real scenario where XLM breaks down even more.

37% is the current recorded loss from its September peak of $0.235. The correction is rapidly erasing the recovery it has been building and finally invalidating it in October. The price is back in the accumulation range that was present back in May.

XLM moves below all major moving averages, with most of them aiming downwards and hinting at the aggravation of the current trend on the market.

With RSI and volume moving sideways or showing a descent, bear trend keeps the upper hand with sustained pressure on the price going further. The only positive signal could be the fact that RSI temporarily violated the reading of 30, which could be the sign of XLM approaching an oversold zone.

From a fundamental perspective, the Bell Protocol upgrade number 30 is the only thing that could set XLM apart from the rest of the market, with the vote scheduled for November 23.

The update will introduce: muxed contract addresses (ability to serve multiple users from a single smart contract), post-quantum signature verification and more.

In order to make the network more responsive, resilient and stronger in the future, millisecond ledger timing is also coming. It won't make the network faster on its own after the upgrade, but it opens up possibilities for developers to improve the network in the future.

#5202. Bybit Says $17.3B Proof of Reserves Covers 50 Assets, but the Audit Has Limits

Strict editorial policy that focuses on accuracy, relevance, and impartiality

Morbi pretium leo et nisl aliquam mollis. Quisque arcu lorem, ultricies quis pellentesque nec, ullamcorper eu odio.

TL;DR: Bybit says the latest snapshot of its proof-of-reserves system covered $17.3 billion in customer assets across 50 cryptocurrencies. The exchange's November 3 announcement describes its 41st report, with figures independently checked by security firm Hacken against an October 27 snapshot.

Bybit says the latest snapshot of its proof-of-reserves system covered $17.3 billion in customer assets across 50 cryptocurrencies. The exchange's November 3 announcement describes its 41st report, with figures independently checked by security firm Hacken against an October 27 snapshot. The exercise offers a view of assets and customer balances at a specific moment, but it should not be mistaken for an all-purpose guarantee that an exchange could meet every liability under every scenario.

Bybit says its reserve ratios exceeded 100% across all 50 covered assets, including 112% for USDT, 405% for USDC, 102% for Bitcoin and 101% for Ether. Those percentages compare identified reserves with customer balances under the stated methodology. An unusually high ratio for one token does not mean the business has the same excess in every asset or that reserves could be freely moved between customer pools. Readers should also distinguish a token-by-token reserve ratio from consolidated corporate solvency, which incorporates obligations that may not appear in a proof-of-reserves snapshot.

The release identifies the audit point as October 27 at 06:00 UTC. Reserve balances, withdrawals, borrowing, market prices and off-exchange exposures may have changed after that time. The value of recurring disclosures lies partly in their comparability: users can look for persistent gaps or unexpected movements rather than treating a single publication as permanent reassurance. Bybit says the checks covered the 50 listed crypto assets, but the exact perimeter, included customer categories and handling of institutional arrangements are questions worth reviewing in the detailed report.

The exchange says Hacken carried out the verification. Third-party involvement is useful, particularly when the reviewer explains its methods and limitations, but the word 'audit' can be interpreted too broadly. A Merkle-tree exercise may allow customers to verify that a balance was included while proving possession of identified onchain assets. It does not, by itself, necessarily establish that there are no undisclosed debts, encumbrances, affiliated-party claims or operational weaknesses. The scope of work therefore deserves as much attention as the name of the verifier.

Asset backing is a fundamental question for custodial exchanges because users depend on the firm to return assets they cannot directly control. Yet security controls, market surveillance, legal ownership of customer property, segregation practices and withdrawal resilience also matter. A fully funded snapshot is only one component of a broader risk picture. The report can be especially informative when combined with clear explanations of liabilities, independent financial statements where available and timely incident reporting. It should not substitute for personal decisions about custodial concentration.

Bybit users can inspect the methodology and verify balances if the relevant tool supports that check. External analysts should compare successive reports and look for consistency between claimed assets and observable onchain holdings. Markets benefit from more systematic disclosures, but transparent limitations are an important part of credible transparency. The milestone is the reporting process, not a declaration that counterparty risk has disappeared.

Primary source: Bybit proof-of-reserves announcement. For additional context, see NewsBTC’s analysis of earnings and stablecoin activity at another major exchange.

#5201. NYSE Selects Avalanche for Tokenized Stock Settlement: Can AVAX Reclaim $9.00?

Home » Altcoins » NYSE Selects Avalanche for Tokenized Stock Settlement: Can AVAX Reclaim $9.00?

Wall Street’s tokenization push gives Avalanche an institutional boost, but AVAX technicals demand caution at important level.

In a major endorsement of enterprise blockchain infrastructure, the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) has chosen Avalanche (AVAX) technology as the core ledger for clearing and settling trades on its planned tokenized stock platform.

According to an Ava Labs statement, Avalanche will provide the core ledger technology for the clearing firm of the NYSE-affiliated digital trading platform.

Subject to regulatory approval, the platform is designed to enable 24/7 trading, clearing and onchain settlement of tokenized securities.

It will support equities issued across multiple blockchains, while Avalanche will provide the underlying settlement infrastructure, offering predictable performance and costs, controlled network access and regulatory flexibility.

The announcement comes amid intensifying competition in financial asset tokenization. The NYSE has expanded its transfer agent network to six firms, including Broadridge, Computershare and Superstate.

Nasdaq is also developing tokenized securities infrastructure, while DTCC launched its tokenization service in October, using a private Besu blockchain and the Canton Network.

Meanwhile, the SEC’s new innovation exemption is opening the door to tokenized equity trading, with OKX and ICE pursuing a separate initiative.

Following the announcement, AVAX broke out of its previous consolidation range but faced resistance at $8.80–$9.00, triggering a pullback. As of November 10, AVAX is trading at approximately $8.48, up 7.2% over 24 hours.

The rally pushed Bollinger Bands wider, signaling increased volatility. AVAX is now trading below the middle band at $8.62 and the moving-average reference at $8.56, suggesting that upward momentum has cooled.

Resistance stands at $8.60–$8.65 and $9.00–$9.28, while key support can be located around $8.00, followed by $7.70 and $7.30.

Over the next few days, AVAX’s direction will largely depend on whether buyers can reclaim $8.65.A recovery above $8.65 could put AVAX back on course to test $9.00. A break above $9.28, supported by stronger trading volume, would signal renewed bullish momentum.

Failure to regain near-term resistance could leave the token consolidating, while a break below $7.95 would expose lower support levels at $7.70 or $7.30.

The NYSE announcement strengthens Avalanche’s position in institutional tokenization, but its implications for AVAX remain less direct. The platform is expected to run on a dedicated blockchain built using Avalanche technology rather than the public Avalanche network.

The extent of any resulting demand for AVAX will depend on how the platform is implemented and whether it creates mechanisms for value to accrue to the token.

A sustained recovery could put $9.50–$10.00 within reach, with further resistance around $10.50–$11.50. However, failure to overcome overhead resistance could leave AVAX in a broader consolidation range.

The NYSE announcement is a significant development for Avalanche’s institutional prospects, but it has yet to translate into a confirmed bullish reversal for AVAX. After the initial AVAX price rally, traders are now assessing whether buying demand can sustain the move beyond the immediate resistance level.

#5200. Coinbase wins final OCC approval – Why COIN stock remains under pressure

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Coinbase has finally received the receipt for the final approval for a national trust bank charter. The Office of the Comptroller of the Currency (OCC), the U.S. regulator responsible for supervising national banks, granted Coinbase final approval last week, following conditional approval in April 2026.

That said, the initial approval meant Coinbase had met certain requirements to move forward, but it still needed to address outstanding regulatory conditions before receiving full authorization. But over the following months, Coinbase took all the steps to satisfy the OCC’s requirements for operating as a national trust bank.

Given the approval, the charter will now allow Coinbase to provide crypto custody services and develop payment-related operations under a federal regulatory framework.

That said, the authorization would also support Coinbase’s stablecoin operations, including issuing tokens and managing stablecoins for other companies. However, the approval does not turn Coinbase into a conventional commercial bank.

It’s important to note that Coinbase National Trust cannot accept deposits from ordinary customers or provide traditional commercial loans.

As of this, Coinbase has now joined Ripple, Revolut, Coinbase, Paxos, BitGo, and Circle, who have already gotten the green light for National Trust Bank. Yet despite this, the stock price of Coinbase ($COIN) was trading at $134.76 after a drop of 1.51% in the past trading day.

This might be because the OCC’s decision to grant national trust bank charters to crypto companies has faced opposition from traditional banking groups. The Independent Community Bankers of America recently sued the regulator, questioning its legal authority to issue certain charters to digital asset firms.

Meanwhile, the SEC too has proposed changes to existing broker-dealer regulations covering capital requirements, customer asset protection, and recordkeeping for firms handling digital assets.

This followed Coinbase securing a legal victory in New Jersey after regulators dropped their lawsuit over its staking services and lifted the cease-and-desist order.

Additionally, Coinbase also recorded $719 million in net income in Q3 2026, compared with $433 million a year earlier. Adjusted EBITDA also reached $333 million, up 60% quarter-over-quarter, marking its 15th consecutive profitable quarter on this metric.

#5199. Publicly Traded Bitcoin Miner Expands BTC Holdings Despite Market Crash

Publicly traded Bitcoin mining giant BitFuFu, based in Singapore, has massively increased its Bitcoin holdings in spite of the current crypto market crash.

Bitcoin Treasuries data shows that the Nasdaq-listed mining firm, trading under the ticker FUFU, added 66 BTC to its Bitcoin holdings. This brings the total Bitcoin held by the company to a massive 1,454 BTC.

As per Bitcoin Treasuries data, BitFuFu now ranks 38th in the Bitcoin 100 following the recent increase in its Bitcoin holdings.

BitFuFu’s increase in Bitcoin holdings comes at a time when the overall cryptocurrency market has been hit by a massive sell-off in the past few days.

However, it is worth noting that the rise in Bitcoin holdings by BitFuFu is as of Oct. 31, before the recent increase in selling pressure in the crypto market.

The Singaporean mining firm reported that it had 1,388 BTC in its holdings by the end of September in its operational update dated Nov. 6. This means its Bitcoin holdings in October showed a 4.8% month-over-month increase.

Three consecutive months of increases in Bitcoin holdings by the Singaporean Bitcoin mining giant are evidence that the company is not backing down from increasing its holdings in the top cryptocurrency.

BitFuFu’s latest increase in its Bitcoin holdings comes amid a massive drop in the company’s mining output.

In its latest operational report, BitFuFu reported that it mined 137 BTC in October compared to 167 BTC mined in September. This shows that the company’s mining output decreased by around 18%.

Out of the total 137 BTC mined in October, 67 BTC were mined through self-mining, while 70 BTC were mined through cloud mining.

The reduction in mining output by BitFuFu was mainly due to a huge increase in Bitcoin mining difficulty, which increased by around 11.8% in October.

However, BitFuFu was able to increase its Bitcoin holdings while keeping its mining power relatively stable.

In October, the total hashrate managed by BitFuFu was 19.5 exahashes per second (EH/s). This is compared to 19.4 EH/s recorded in September.

BitFuFu Chairman and CEO Leo Lu reiterates commitment to increasing Bitcoin holdings.

#5198. Solana Changelog Puts Faster Web3.js Builds and Validator Work in Focus

Strict editorial policy that focuses on accuracy, relevance, and impartiality

Morbi pretium leo et nisl aliquam mollis. Quisque arcu lorem, ultricies quis pellentesque nec, ullamcorper eu odio.

TL;DR: Solana's November 5 developer changelog highlights a collection of tooling and validator improvements rather than one headline-grabbing network upgrade. Among them are faster builds for JavaScript applications, changes affecting validator operations and further work around execution tools.

Solana's November 5 developer changelog highlights a collection of tooling and validator improvements rather than one headline-grabbing network upgrade. Among them are faster builds for JavaScript applications, changes affecting validator operations and further work around execution tools. For developers, these smaller improvements can influence how quickly applications ship and how reliably infrastructure behaves. For investors, the distinction is that a published development update is not necessarily a newly activated change on mainnet.

One item in the changelog concerns the JavaScript SDK build process using Rolldown. The developers describe a reduction in an internal build benchmark from around two seconds to almost zero. That is a promising productivity claim, but it applies to the specified workflow and benchmark rather than every Solana app suddenly loading faster for users. Faster library builds can improve testing and release cycles, particularly for teams maintaining many packages. Its end-user effect depends on whether downstream developers adopt the updated tools and whether the gains carry over to their own projects.

The update also points to work on the Agave validator client, typed interfaces and elements of Firedancer leader handover. These terms describe software and operational changes within the infrastructure that keeps the chain running. Their practical significance lies in reliability, interoperability and the conditions under which different validator implementations coordinate. However, a changelog reference should not be treated as evidence that every validator has deployed the work or that a planned network feature has passed all activation checks. Operators need specific release notes, configuration guidance and rollout schedules.

The changelog includes improvements involving Anchor, RPC-related tools and local development environments such as Surfpool. Anchor is widely used for building Solana programs, while RPC services provide the interface through which wallets, apps and analytics products query the chain. Small optimizations in account allocation or local testing can save developer time and reduce avoidable mistakes. They do not remove the need for security review, careful transaction handling or capacity planning. Because Solana applications rely on several layers, a weakness at the RPC or front-end level can still affect users even when core consensus works normally.

Solana has long emphasized throughput and latency, but engineering benchmarks depend on hardware, software versions and the specific task measured. An SDK build benchmark is not a transaction-per-second statistic, while validator handover work is not a promise of zero outages. Keeping those categories separate helps readers understand what the team has actually improved. Adoption may eventually show up in developer retention, easier deployment and more dependable applications, but those effects take time and should be measured rather than assumed from a release note.

Developers can follow the linked repositories, testing results and future rollout notices for each change. The clearest evidence of impact would be adoption in major tools, reproducible performance measurements and fewer operational headaches for app builders and node operators. The November update reflects continuous ecosystem maintenance—often less dramatic than a large protocol announcement, but essential to whether Solana can support more demanding financial applications over time.

Primary source: Solana developer changelog. For additional context, see NewsBTC’s recent review of Solana’s October development milestones.

#5197. Critical Ethereum Security Alert Issued Over Compromised Software

Blockchain security provider SlowMist has issued an urgent security alert for Ethereum ecosystem users after a complex supply chain attack was detected on Ethersphere's software.

In a recent post on the X social media platform, SlowMist announced that two pieces of software have been compromised by hackers.

The cybersecurity firm claims that attackers were able to break into GitHub accounts and take advantage of legitimate software release processes to deploy malicious software.

The attack could potentially impact users on multiple operating systems, including Windows, macOS and Linux.

The affected software is part of Swarm, a decentralized storage network belonging to the wider Ethereum ecosystem.

Security experts have cautioned that these malicious software releases could enable attackers to compromise cryptocurrency wallet credentials, steal private keys and open up permanent backdoors on affected systems.

One of the most concerning features of this attack is the involvement of Ethereum's blockchain infrastructure in helping manage the malicious software.

SlowMist explains that this malware retrieves and runs malicious JavaScript code.

However, this attack is not just a simple malware infection.

The cybersecurity firm disclosed that attackers are leveraging an Ethereum smart contract as a decentralized command-and-control (C2) configuration repository.

This gives attackers the ability to dynamically update server addresses managing the malware without requiring a new deployment of the malicious software.

Interestingly, the same Ethereum smart contract was previously associated with the infamous SleepyDuck and EtherDuck malware attacks. These campaigns targeted developers using malicious extensions on the Open VSX marketplace.

This points to the possibility that the new attack may be part of a broader operation aimed at compromising cryptocurrency developers and users.

Independent security researchers from Socket and SafeDep also discovered malware aimed at stealing Swarm wallet files and deploying persistent remote access tools.

As a result of this discovery, SlowMist advised users to immediately uninstall the affected package and roll back to the clean version 13.2.0.

In the meantime, users of Swarm Desktop v0.56.2 were advised to update to the clean v0.56.3 software release.

#5196. Circle Makes the Case for AI Agent Payments, With Spending Controls at the Center

Strict editorial policy that focuses on accuracy, relevance, and impartiality

Morbi pretium leo et nisl aliquam mollis. Quisque arcu lorem, ultricies quis pellentesque nec, ullamcorper eu odio.

TL;DR: Circle is arguing that stablecoins could become a useful payment layer for AI agents that buy software, access services or settle transactions on behalf of people and businesses. A November 4 explainer from the USDC issuer sets out what it calls agentic commerce, a concept that would allow authorized software to initiate purchases within predefined rules.

Circle is arguing that stablecoins could become a useful payment layer for AI agents that buy software, access services or settle transactions on behalf of people and businesses. A November 4 explainer from the USDC issuer sets out what it calls agentic commerce, a concept that would allow authorized software to initiate purchases within predefined rules. It is a vision for a developing market, not evidence that an autonomous AI payments network has already reached mass adoption.

The basic idea is to let a software agent complete a purchase after receiving instructions and permission from a human or business. An agent might compare pricing, obtain a computing service, pay a provider and record the transaction without asking a person to click through each step. This differs from a chatbot that only recommends products or from an automatic recurring subscription. Stablecoins are appealing in this setting because value can move over internet-native rails and settle across borders, but those features are useful only when an agent can prove what it is authorized to do.

The central design challenge is delegated authority. A business needs to specify which agent may spend, how much, on which categories of supplier and for what period. It also needs authentication, records of the instruction that triggered a payment and a way to revoke authority. Without those safeguards, automated payments can turn minor software errors or malicious prompts into real financial losses. Circle's discussion points to the infrastructure opportunity, but a stablecoin transfer alone cannot determine whether the underlying purchase was legitimate or met the user's expectations.

USDC and other dollar-linked tokens could allow small cross-border payments and programmable transfers that are difficult to reproduce with traditional card infrastructure. However, blockchain transfers can be difficult to reverse. Conventional card payments often involve dispute rights, fraud monitoring and customer-support processes that do not automatically accompany an onchain transfer. A workable agent payment service therefore needs explicit handling for refunds, incorrect goods, failed delivery and disputes. It must also address sanctions checks, identity requirements and jurisdictional limits where relevant. Technology that moves money quickly cannot replace those business obligations.

Developers are experimenting with AI systems that interact with tools, APIs and online marketplaces. As those systems gain the ability to perform tasks rather than simply produce text, a reliable method of paying for digital services becomes more relevant. Stablecoin issuers and payment processors see potential demand, but practical uptake depends on merchants accepting the method and users trusting the controls. The most credible demonstrations will involve narrow, verifiable use cases rather than broad claims that intelligent agents can safely manage unlimited funds.

Payment providers will need to show who bears liability when an agent makes a mistaken purchase, how permissions are audited and how customers recover from fraud. They will also need to show cost advantages after fees, compliance and integration work are included. Circle's essay is best read as an articulation of a developing architecture, not a new product launch. The long-term test is whether automated payments become easier for ordinary businesses without making financial control harder.

Primary source: Circle analysis of agentic commerce. For additional context, see NewsBTC’s report on a consumer-facing USDC payment integration.

#5195. Hyperliquid loses KEY support – Can a bear trap send HYPE to $100?

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Is Hyperliquid’s [HYPE] current breakdown a potential bear trap before a $100 breakout?

Notably, analysts are not convinced. According to one analyst, Hyperliquid is breaking out of its rectangular consolidation pattern over the last 2 months. The key level to watch here is $75. If HYPE cannot hold this, then $64 is the next key level to watch, followed by $53. However, with HYPE already trading below the $75 level following a 2% intraday dip, the technical picture is becoming more bearish.

On top of this, Lookonchain highlighted that a whale has unstaked 512,010 HYPE tokens worth approximately $39.3 million and deposited them into FalconX, possibly to sell. In essence, weakening price action and a large whale transfer are adding to bearish pressure.

Naturally, this raises the question: Is a drop toward $53 becoming more likely?

Binance has now reopened the withdrawal of HYPE tokens after the $6.3 billion hack. This means that users will be able to move HYPE off the exchange. If withdrawals pick up, exchange balances could decline, potentially tightening the available supply and creating conditions for a supply squeeze.

So, if this trend holds, could bulls flip the bearish Hyperliquid setup into a classic bear trap and catch late shorts off guard? With overall market sentiment in extreme fear, an unexpected reversal could trigger a sharp short squeeze, potentially setting HYPE up for a repeat of its late-July rally.

HYPE’s historical price action suggests another rally toward price discovery cannot be ruled out.

Following a dip back to $50 in mid-Q2, HYPE showed some serious strength, moving into price discovery territory and hitting an all-time high of almost $95. With a similar technical setup starting to take shape, another rally remains possible. The fact that Binance has reopened withdrawals will also help in terms of creating this price discovery condition by allowing tokens to move off exchanges.

On-chain activity is also providing a bullish signal. According to On-chain Lens, in the past 24 hours, Hyperliquid has burned $1.06 million of HYPE. This is a buyback process where HYPE is purchased using revenue from the protocol. The HYPE is then destroyed and removed completely from circulation.

More importantly, the pace of buybacks could increase if protocol revenue continues to rise.

As shown on the chart, Hyperliquid’s revenue has risen by 20% in under 24 hours, adding nearly $290k. Higher revenue could translate into larger buybacks, which can increase the amount of HYPE being removed from supply. This can provide further support for a recovery if the altcoin begins to see a rise in demand.

This is where HYPE’s historical price action and Binance’s withdrawal reopening become important. The continued buybacks and possible shortages mean a drop down to $53 is too much of a stretch. Instead, the breakdown could result in a bear trap, which catches late shorts and creates a July-style rally. Thus, a move toward $100 could still be on the cards for November.

#5194. Bitcoin (BTC) Capitulation: Retail Holders Are Melting, Dropping BTC Off

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

Bitcoin has been losing wallets on the network since October 16 of this year. With the number of non-empty wallets peaking at 59.99 million that day, the network has already lost roughly 108,700 of them as the price dropped back below $75,000. Smaller holders are clearly running out of patience.

Bitcoin's last bullish hope was a short recovery rally towards the $80,000–$81,000 price threshold in early November. It failed to hold on to that momentum and quickly tumbled towards the $76,000 area, which opened the way towards more losses. Since its October top near $87,000, BTC lost roughly 14% of its value and lost everything it built up since late August.

BTC moves below all major moving averages, with most of them aiming downwards and hinting at the aggravation of the current trend on the market. RSI confirms the thesis, moving around 40 and suggesting a bearish momentum, or a sideways movement at least.

Fueled by the falling price, retail is still fearful: smaller traders are merging or emptying their wallets. The network now holds around 59.88 million non-empty wallets, while exchange supply is down near 1.28 million BTC. The shrinking wallet count, however, isn't something that automatically means Bitcoin is being used less.

But where would BTC finally settle down? Realistically, the closest 'fair' support is the $74,000–$75,000 area, where buyers already stepped in this week. Discussing a breakdown below makes little sense, unfortunately, due to the volatile nature of the market.

But even if BTC holds the $74,000 support, bulls might still stay sidelined. There are little to no signs of retail coming back that can help BTC reach key market levels. Even worse, the market in general isn't favoring bulls, so bracing yourself for a prolonged correction might be the wise choice.

In case bulls somehow wake up, a recovery towards $78,000–$80,000 is the main priority, as it's the area where several moving averages are sitting right now. A falling wallet count with the price holding often acts as a sign of sellers getting exhausted, as smaller investors tend to give up when fear is at its highest.

Whether buyers can defend the $74,000 level or not is the main test for the asset right now. The wallet count keeps shrinking, the price is stuck below the moving averages and a short-lived price bounce here won't change the situation as a whole, unfortunately.

#5192. Valour Lists Eight New Crypto ETPs Including Canton, Morpho and Raydium

Strict editorial policy that focuses on accuracy, relevance, and impartiality

Morbi pretium leo et nisl aliquam mollis. Quisque arcu lorem, ultricies quis pellentesque nec, ullamcorper eu odio.

TL;DR: Valour, a subsidiary of DeFi Technologies, says it has added eight digital asset exchange-traded products to Sweden's Spotlight Stock Market. The November 5 announcement expands the issuer's range beyond the most established cryptocurrencies into a mixture of decentralized finance, blockchain infrastructure and speculative community tokens.

Valour, a subsidiary of DeFi Technologies, says it has added eight digital asset exchange-traded products to Sweden's Spotlight Stock Market. The November 5 announcement expands the issuer's range beyond the most established cryptocurrencies into a mixture of decentralized finance, blockchain infrastructure and speculative community tokens. The listings widen the menu available through a securities-market wrapper, but the variety also makes it important for investors to understand what each product actually tracks.

Valour's new SEK-denominated products reference Venice Token (VVV), Canton Coin (CC), Raydium (RAY), Morpho (MORPHO), Maple (SYRUP), Pump.fun (PUMP), Dogwifhat (WIF) and Bonk (BONK). They represent very different markets. Some connect to lending, trading or institutional blockchain networks, while others derive demand from online communities and token speculation. Presenting all eight as one diversified crypto strategy would be misleading. A buyer of one listed product is exposed to its specific token and to the terms of the ETP, not to a balanced basket of the eight assets.

An exchange-traded product can give eligible investors exposure through a brokerage account rather than requiring them to maintain a wallet and manage token transfers. That can simplify record keeping and access within markets where the product is available. It does not necessarily provide the same legal structure or protections as a conventional equity fund, however. Investors should review whether the product is physically backed, how custody is handled, what fees are charged, and whether creation and redemption work efficiently. The securities wrapper can reduce some operational burdens while leaving underlying crypto price risk very much intact.

Canton and Morpho are associated with institutional blockchain and DeFi infrastructure, Raydium with Solana-based trading and Maple with onchain credit. By contrast, meme-linked tokens such as Dogwifhat and Bonk have historically depended heavily on market sentiment. Pump.fun exposure carries its own commercial and regulatory questions. None of these classifications determines future performance, but the differences matter for portfolio construction and due diligence. The existence of a regulated trading venue for an ETP is not an official endorsement of the underlying token's economic value.

The products are quoted in SEK, so an investor whose reference currency is different must consider exchange-rate moves as well as the token price. Trading hours and liquidity may also differ from around-the-clock cryptocurrency markets. That mismatch can create wider spreads or price gaps when significant token moves occur while the securities venue is closed. Buyers should examine the issuer's market-making arrangements and the gap between quoted prices and indicative net asset values rather than assuming that an exchange listing always guarantees a tight execution price.

Valour's expansion shows how issuers are testing demand for more specialized crypto exposures through familiar financial channels. Whether the new products become meaningful investment options will depend on trading volumes, spreads, fees, custody disclosures and sustained interest beyond launch week. For market observers, the story is less about eight fresh tickers than the effort to package increasingly niche digital assets for traditional securities accounts—and the safeguards needed when investors follow them there.

Primary source: DeFi Technologies / Valour product announcement. For additional context, see NewsBTC’s analysis of trading flows through established Bitcoin funds.
